user
Blue Dragon GPU HLE in xenia (Vulkan), PC-first (desktop --gpu=vulkan, pure-LLE path). Goal: delete the EDRAM color ownership transfers by making the composite sample the NATIVE field/bloom images instead of the EDRAM-resolved textures. Answer concretely.

WORKING: I render BD's color producers (field, bloom, composite) each into a PRIVATE native VkImage (keyed by resolve-dest = D3D9 resource identity), keep LLE depth, seed LLE->native, render, mirror native->LLE. PRESENT reads the native composite image directly and is PIXEL-PERFECT (the swap gamma pass uses the logical 1280x720 size). Confirmed correct on desktop.

BROKEN (the sampler redirect): I made the composite's pixel-shader texture fetches of the field/bloom bind my native image (via rt_as_texture_views_pixel_ override + a swizzle-aware VkImageView using the guest texture swizzle). The redirect FIRES for all the right bases. But the composite renders GARBAGE (mostly black + a garbled band). Two identified problems:

1) EXTENT/UV MISMATCH: my native image is the RENDER TARGET, allocated at the EDRAM-tile-rounded size (e.g. field = 720x1824, bloom similar tall). But the composite samples it as a LOGICAL-size TEXTURE (e.g. 720x720) with normalized [0,1] UVs. A VkImageView can't crop, so [0,1] maps to the full 1824-tall image -> the shader reads mostly the empty tail region -> black/garbage. (The PRESENT path avoids this because the gamma pass is told the logical size 1280x720, not the image size.)

2) SHARED IMAGE REUSE: the native image is ONE per framebuffer, reused every pass/frame (seed discards + re-renders). BD re-begins the same color framebuffer multiple times per frame, so by the time the composite samples "the field", the shared native image may hold a later pass's content, not the field's.

QUESTIONS:
1) EXTENT: what's the cleanest fix so the composite samples the correct [0, logical_height] region of my tile-rounded native RT image? Options I see: (a) allocate the native image at the LOGICAL resolve size instead of tile-rounded (but the render pass framebuffer needs the RT dims - does the guest actually render beyond logical rows?); (b) make a separate logical-size SAMPLED copy of the native image at publish time (an extra blit, but correct dims); (c) scale the UVs in the shader (needs shader modification per-fetch - probably too invasive); (d) something else. Which is correct + minimal? Note the guest texture the composite samples has a logical size (from the texture fetch constant) that I can read.
2) REUSE/GENERATION: to guarantee the composite samples the FIELD's content (not a later same-framebuffer pass), do I need a per-generation image pool (retire by submission), or is there a simpler invariant? The present path works with one image because it samples at frame end after the last write; the sampler samples mid-frame. What's the minimal correct lifetime scheme for the sampled case?
3) Given (1)+(2), is the SAMPLED-native approach (drop the field/bloom->composite transfers) worth the complexity, or is there a simpler equivalent that still deletes those EDRAM transfers on a TBDR (the real target is the Adreno tile-store; PC has fence_us=2us so no PC perf signal - PC is only for correctness)? E.g. is keeping the composite on LLE-sampled EDRAM but making the field/bloom RESOLVES cheap/native a better lever than redirecting the composite's samplers?

Be concrete about the extent fix (option a/b/c) and the generation-pool minimal design.

# Xbox 360 D3D-HLE + AOT-recompile knowledge base

## THE STRATEGIC MODEL (proven by UnleashedRecomp)
Stop emulating the Xbox 360 GPU at the PM4 command level (xenia LLE) ƒ?" that faithfully reproduces the
game's GPU inefficiencies. Instead **translate the D3D API to Vulkan directly (HLE)** + **AOT-recompile
the PPC to native**. hedge-dev's stack is the reference implementation:

- **XenonRecomp** (../reference/XenonRecomp) ƒ?" Xbox360 PPC XEX -> C++.
  - CPU state struct (`PPCContext`, XenonUtils/ppc_context.h) passed to EVERY recompiled fn; 2nd arg = base
    pointer (guest 32-bit ptrs). Instructions operate little-endian; loads/stores swap endian (marked
    volatile to stop unsafe Clang reordering). Vector regs: the WHOLE 16-byte vector is reversed (use WZY
    not XYZ in dot products, reverse args for pack). FPU keeps denormals; VMX flushes them (FP-state flag
    in the context toggled per instruction).
  - **VMX via x86 intrinsics; ARM64 via SIMDe (simd-everywhere)** => THE ARM64 PORT IS NOT FROM SCRATCH.
  - Indirect/virtual calls: perfect-hash table (fn addr*2 -> recompiled fn ptr), placed after the XEX
    region in base memory; macros in generated `ppc_config.h`.
  - Jump tables: detected by `mtctr r0` + `bctr` patterns; XenonAnalyse emits a TOML; per-game tuning.
  - Function boundaries: `.pdata` segment + heuristics; manual overrides in TOML.
  - **NO runtime provided** (you write kernel HLE + renderer + input + audio). NO exceptions. NO MMIO/XMA
    (unimplemented ƒ?" but xenia HAS an XMA decoder + full kernel HLE to port/reference).
- **XenosRecomp** (../reference/XenosRecomp) ƒ?" Xenos shader bytecode -> HLSL -> DXIL/SPIR-V (via DXC).
  - Vertex fetch: Xbox vertex DECLARATIONS -> native D3D12/Vulkan INPUT LAYOUTS (no runtime shader
    permutations); endian-swaps vertex data; 16-bit swizzle fix + R11G11B10 unpack via spec constants.
  - Constant buffers: 3 slots (VS 256 float4, PS 224 float4, shared) -> root cbuffers (D3D12) / push
    constants (Vulkan), populated from shader reflection.
  - **BYPASSES tiling / EDRAM / resolve ENTIRELY** ƒ?" the translation layer never reproduces them. THIS is
    exactly why it avoids BD's foliage-binning-doubling: it renders the scene ONCE, optimally.
- **UnleashedRecomp** (Sonic Unleashed) = the full proven port; renderer = "translation layer, not GPU
  emulation". **ReXGlue** = the same static-recompile lineage the /goal names.

## WHY THIS KILLS BD's BOTTLENECK (rigorous, from this campaign)
BD's field is GPU-bound (cpu_real 2.9ms vs 52ms GPU-fence-wait) on **foliage TRIANGLE-BINNING doubled by
predicated tiling** (2 tile passes; host_draws 940 vs rendered 675). Every xenia-LLE lever failed:
fill/MSAA/resolution (not fill-bound), naive flatten (tiles are pre-offset by the D3D RUNTIME at replay =
can't merge emulator-side), merge_draws (inapplicable), deint (inert), decimation (~25fps + artifacts,
thins foliage). HLE renders the scene ONCE (no tiling) => bin-once by construction, full foliage, correct
pixels. AOT is ORTHOGONAL: helps CPU-bound titles (Burnout/Gears) + heat; BD field GPU-bound => AOT is
thermal-only there.

## HYBRID PLAN (user-chosen 2026-07-02)
Keep xenia as the runtime base; build a D3D-HLE renderer path INSIDE it incrementally; share our existing
LLVM whole-fn backend (src/xenia/cpu/backend/llvm) as the AOT foundation. Two parallel tracks:
- TACTICAL (now): the one-tile guest patch (task #39) ƒ?" a SCOPED HLE of just BD's tiling (force tile
  count->1). Near-term correct-pixel 30fps shot within xenia.
- STRATEGIC: study how UnleashedRecomp's renderer maps Xenos draws -> Vulkan and design an xenia HLE path
  (intercept D3D draw/setrendertarget/tiling at the guest-call level instead of PM4). Start with the
  smallest surface (the tiling + main draw path) and grow.

## BD TILING MACHINERY (RE'd this campaign ƒ?" for the one-tile patch)
- Walker `0x82487878`: reads tile count `*(FUN_826bfc68()+4)`, walks cmd-list segments, per draw computes
  a per-tile predication mask vs a tile-rect table at `state+0xC` (16B/tile), writes `mask|0x80000000` to
  draw+8. (This fn CRASHED the resolution patches @0x82487948 ƒ?" delicate; changing dims desyncs the table.)
- Tiling loop `0x82487978`: gets state via `0x826BFC7C`, calls the walker per segment.
- Emitter `0x82473BD8`: writes SET_BIN_MASK/SELECT PM4 (0xc0006000/6200/6100/6300); this one resets to
  all-bins 0xffffffff. Per-tile selects (0x80000003, 0x0C) emitted in the loop.
- PATCH TARGET: where the tiling SETUP writes state+4 = tile count (2) + state+0xC = rects. Force count=1 +
  full-screen rect. NOT YET PINNED ƒ?" trace who writes state+4; state getter FUN_826bfc68 is a large fn.

## KNOWLEDGE SOURCES (the "comprehensive RAG")
- Reference source (cloned): ../reference/XenonRecomp, ../reference/XenosRecomp. (UnleashedRecomp = the
  renderer reference; clone hedge-dev/UnleashedRecomp when building the HLE renderer.)
- Xenos GPU / PM4 command reference: IN xenia's own source ƒ?" src/xenia/gpu/xenos.h (PM4 opcodes),
  packet_disassembler.cc, registers.h, the vulkan/ translator. This IS the authoritative PM4/EDRAM/resolve
  reference.
- Xbox 360 D3D9 API (XDK): the guest D3D is statically linked in each XEX (0x8246xxxx region for BD). No
  public XDK redistribution; reconstruct the API shapes from RE + the Free60/libxenon community docs +
  XenonRecomp's handling. D3DDevice_* ordinals.
- This campaign's RE artifacts: scratch/thor-debug/bd_*.txt (walker/tiling/resflow/modeflag decompiles),
  scratch/ghidra/scripts/Bd*.java.

## WORKFLOW
- RE a game's tiling/surface: Ghidra headless (see xenia-thor-ghidra-game-patch skill) on the XEX; find
  SET_BIN_SELECT emitter via the PM4 header constants (ori 0x6200/6300/6000/6100); decompile the walker +
  its callers; trace the tile-count writer.
- Design an HLE intercept: identify the guest D3D fn (statically linked, by ordinal/behavior), decide
  whether to (a) PATCH it (.patch.toml, cheapest ƒ?" e.g. tile count->1), or (b) HLE-replace it (patch entry
  to trampoline into a host impl ƒ?" the real translation layer).
- Validate: device A/B (single-run alternating for perf; screenshot for pixels); the alternator-gated
  method (gpu_freeze_ab_alternate_vrs phase) is the rigorous perf harness.

## ƒo. PROVEN REƒ+'HLE RECIPE (2026-07-03) ƒ?" reusable across games, this is THE fast path
The "reach in, pin the D3D fn, HLE it" loop, validated on BD's tiling replay. Four committed tools:

**1. PIN the D3D fn that writes a target struct ƒ?" PAGE-WATCH (`cpu_watch_guest_write_page`, a64_backend.cc
ExceptionCallback + processor.cc walker-protect).** The store-watch (`arm64_guest_store_watch`) FAILS on the
field (per-store JIT emit = ~10x slowdown ƒ+' timed nav desyncs). The page-watch has ZERO per-store cost: host-
mprotect the target guest page read-only; on write-fault, **emulate-on-fault** (decode store size[31:30]+Rt
[4:0], do the write to ex->fault_address() yourself, keep protected = no un-protect windows = catch EVERY
write) + `code_cache->LookupFunction(ex->pc())` ƒ+' the writer guest fn + `ctx->lr` (PPCContext @ host x[20]) ƒ+'
its CALLER. Runs BD full-speed ƒ+' reaches the field. THIS is the tool the multi-session RE wall needed.

**2. Peel helper wrappers.** Writers are often memcpy(0x826bf770)/calloc(0x8246B3B8) helpers; the real D3D fn
is the CALLER ƒ?" read `ctx->lr` in the page-watch hit. calloc/memcpy have NO static refs (deep indirect) so
static Ghidra caller-analysis is DEAD; the runtime page-watch lr is the only way.

**3. GOTCHA ƒ?" xenia's DECLARED entry ƒ%ÿ Ghidra prologue.** BD's tiling replay: Ghidra prologue-scan said
0x82487cc8, but xenia harvested it as **0x82487CC0-82487F50** (grep logcat `shared-fn-harvest`). HLE the
xenia-DECLARED entry (0x82487CC0), else `IsHleIntercept` never matches ƒ+' planted=0. Also: fns reached by
tail-call/indirect (driver 0x82487988) are NEVER LookupFunction'd ƒ+' un-plantable; only harvested entries plant.

**4. HLE HOST BODY ƒ?" reimplement the D3D fn (`cpu_hle_tiling_replay_addr`, processor.cc HleTilingReplayHandler).**
SetupExtern at LookupFunction plants the host handler (kExtern dispatch, no guest-code patch). For a REPLAY/
token-loop fn, reimplement the loop in host C++, reading the recorded stream (r3) + ctx (r4) with load/store_
and_swap (BE), and calling guest helpers (ring writer 0x8246E100, tiling helpers) REENTRANTLY via
`Processor::Execute(ThreadState::Get(), addr, args[], n)` (argsƒ+'r[3+i], returns r[3]; keep your loop state in
C++ LOCALS since Execute clobbers ctx->r). VIABLE: only ~dozens of setup PM4 emits/frame (draws go via the
driver/walker, not the tiling-setup fn). Device: **planted+faults=0 = the host body IS the D3D fn + runs**.
Then apply the bin-once tweak (force tile count=1) + the COHERENT surface rewrite (RB_SURFACE_INFO pitch +
color/depth EDRAM bases + resolve TOGETHER ƒ?" piecemeal downstream override = 0fps black EDRAM desync; that's
why HLE-at-SOURCE beats RT-cache coalescing). VdGlobalDevice ptr = *(0x820005f4).

## dY?-‹,? LOAD-TIME XEX PIPELINE (RPCS3-model endgame ƒ?" the user's "progress bar" vision)
The right architecture = at GAME LOAD, a progress-bar pass that (a) AOT-LLVM-compiles the PPC (we HAVE the
object cache: [[llvm-jit-backend-build]], `cpu_backend_llvm` + AOT .o cache), and (b) SIGNATURE-scans the XEX
for the static-linked XDK D3D9 fns ƒ+' installs HLE trampolines (the mechanism above), caching both keyed by
XEX hash. RPCS3 analog: its boot progress bar AOT-recompiles PPU/SPU modules; we AOT the PPC + (the SPUƒ+'HLE
analog) signature-identify + host-replace the D3D fns. This = build-order step 4 (a signature DB stable per
XDK version 2005-2013, generic across games ƒ?" NOT a per-game port). EDRAM is THE core inefficiency; HLE-ing
BeginTiling/Resolve bypasses the predicated tiling + EDRAM round-trip AT THE SOURCE = render once / resolve
once. To generalize: the page-watch recipe pins BeginTiling/Resolve/Draw per game; their SIGNATURES (byte
patterns of the harvested fn) go in the DB; the loader matches + trampolines them without re-RE.

name: xenia-windows-powershell-command-hygiene
description: Use whenever Codex runs shell commands in xenia-thor on Windows PowerShell, edits PowerShell scripts, composes Git/ADB/Gradle/Thor helper commands, or recovers from shell syntax failures such as Bash-style &&, ||, quoting, redirection, or mixed-shell path handling.

# Xenia Windows PowerShell Command Hygiene

This repo usually runs through Windows PowerShell 5.1. Treat it as PowerShell,
not Bash, unless the tool explicitly says otherwise.

## Hard Rules

- Do not use Bash command chaining such as `&&` or `||`.
- Do not assume Bash quoting, globbing, subshells, here-docs, `export`, `touch`,
  `cat > file`, `rm -rf`, or `/c/...` paths.
- Prefer one command per tool call. For independent reads, use
  `multi_tool_use.parallel`; for dependent steps, run a second command after
  checking the first result.
- When a single PowerShell call really needs multiple dependent steps, use
  newline-separated PowerShell statements plus explicit failure checks:

```powershell
git status --short
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
git branch --show-current
if ($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) { exit $LASTEXITCODE }
```

- Run repo PowerShell scripts with:

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\thor\script.ps1 -Mode Example
```

- For Android Java/XML/resource-only launcher changes, prefer the fast shell
  build first:

```powershell
powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tools\thor\thor_build.ps1 -Mode ApkShell -NoSubst
```

  Use `-NoSubst` for this UI loop if Gradle resource merge fails only through
  the temporary no-space drive with `!directory.isDirectory`; reserve the
  default subst path for native builds that still need no-space protection.

- Quote paths with spaces by passing arguments, `-LiteralPath`, or call
  operator syntax:

```powershell
Get-Content -LiteralPath "C:\path with spaces\file.txt" -Raw
& "C:\path with spaces\script.ps1" -Mode Status
```

- Do not pipe generated path strings into destructive commands. Resolve and
  verify paths first; use native PowerShell cmdlets with `-LiteralPath`.
- Avoid variable names that differ only by case from built-ins. For example,
  `$HOME` is read-only and PowerShell variables are case-insensitive, so `$home`
  will fail too; use names like `$codexHome` or `$repoRoot`. `$PID` is also
  read-only; use names like `$processIdText` or `$targetPid`.
- Use `Set-Content` / `Add-Content` only for generated artifacts when
  `apply_patch` is not the right tool. For repo edits, use `apply_patch`.
- For ADB commands, pass arguments as arrays when scripting and include
  `-DeviceSerial` / `adb -s <serial>` whenever more than one device could exist.
- For screenshots and binary ADB streams, use the repo helper or a known-safe
  `cmd /c "adb exec-out screencap -p > file.png"` pattern; do not pipe binary
  PNG data through PowerShell text cmdlets.

## Common Replacements

- `cmd1 && cmd2` -> two tool calls, or newline plus `$LASTEXITCODE` checks.
- `grep` -> `Select-String`; prefer `rg` for repository text search.
- `cat file` -> `Get-Content -Raw file`.
- `ls` -> `Get-ChildItem`.
- `rm -rf path` -> `Remove-Item -LiteralPath path -Recurse -Force` only after
  confirming the resolved path is inside the intended workspace.
- `VAR=value command` -> `$env:VAR = "value"` before the command, then restore
  or remove it if needed.

## Before Committing

If this skill was updated because of a command failure, leave a dated worklog
entry naming the failure class and the guardrail added. Validate with
`git diff --check`; PowerShell CRLF warnings alone are acceptable for this repo.

codex
The current tree confirms this is already beyond the old “aux RT” experiment: the private-color path and native texture override are present, while depth stays LLE. I’m now tracing three exact contracts in the code: the native attachment extent, the resolve-to-resource publication point, and whether a later write can mutate an image already referenced by an earlier composite descriptor.

diff --git a/src/xenia/gpu/render_target_cache.h b/src/xenia/gpu/render_target_cache.h
index 24fe40dd1..2740a3bd6 100644
--- a/src/xenia/gpu/render_target_cache.h
+++ b/src/xenia/gpu/render_target_cache.h
@@ -205,6 +205,19 @@ class RenderTargetCache {
   uint32_t GetLastUpdateBoundRenderTargets(
       uint32_t* depth_and_color_formats_out = nullptr) const;
 
+  // The packed RenderTargetKey of the current draw's color render target
+  // `color_index` (0-based), or 0 if none is bound. This is the SAME full identity
+  // (base+pitch+format+msaa) the resolve path records, letting the native-HLE
+  // render-redirect key a bound RT to its resolve-dest surface unambiguously —
+  // EDRAM base alone aliases (BD renders every RT at base 0).
+  uint32_t GetLastUpdateColorRenderTargetKey(uint32_t color_index) const {
+    if (color_index >= xenos::kMaxColorRenderTargets) {
+      return 0;
+    }
+    const RenderTarget* rt = last_update_used_render_targets_[1 + color_index];
+    return rt ? rt->key().key : 0;
+  }
+
  protected:
   RenderTargetCache(const RegisterFile& register_file, const Memory& memory,
                     TraceWriter* trace_writer, uint32_t draw_resolution_scale_x,

-bool BdNativeRenderer::Initialize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height) {
+// Desktop Vulkan (NVIDIA/AMD/Intel) is lenient about render-pass<->pipeline
+// compatibility; the Adreno driver (Qualcomm proprietary vendorID 0x5143, and
+// Mesa Turnip) is strict and CRASHES (null-deref inside vulkan.adreno.so) on the
+// native renderer's mismatched pass. This is THE fork point for device-specific
+// (Adreno super-optimized) vs desktop (structure/correctness) code paths.
+bool BdNativeRenderer::IsAdreno() const {
+  const auto* dev = command_processor_.GetVulkanDevice();
+  if (!dev) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  const auto& props = dev->properties();
+  return props.vendorID == 0x5143u ||  // Qualcomm
+         std::strstr(props.deviceName, "Adreno") != nullptr ||
+         std::strstr(props.deviceName, "Turnip") != nullptr;
+}

+bool BdNativeRenderer::ResolveMsaa(DeferredCommandBuffer& command_buffer) {
+  if (!IsAdreno() || samples_ == VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT ||
+      resolve_image_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE || color_image_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    return false;  // desktop uses the in-pass resolve; single-sample = nothing.
+  }
+  // color_image_ is COLOR_ATTACHMENT (render pass finalLayout for MSAA). Resolve
+  // the multisampled color -> single-sample resolve_image_ (what the in-pass resolve
+  // does on desktop), leaving resolve_image_ SHADER_READ for present / stretch.
+  VkImageSubresourceRange range = {};
+  range.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
+  range.levelCount = 1;
+  range.layerCount = 1;
+
+  VkImageMemoryBarrier pre[2] = {};
+  pre[0].sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
+  pre[0].srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_WRITE_BIT;
+  pre[0].dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_READ_BIT;
+  pre[0].oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL;
+  pre[0].newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL;
+  pre[0].srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  pre[0].dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  pre[0].image = color_image_;
+  pre[0].subresourceRange = range;
+  pre[1] = pre[0];
+  pre[1].srcAccessMask = 0;
+  pre[1].dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT;
+  pre[1].oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
+  pre[1].newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL;
+  pre[1].image = resolve_image_;
+  command_buffer.CmdVkPipelineBarrier(
+      VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OUTPUT_BIT |
+          VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT,
+      VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT, 0, 0, nullptr, 0, nullptr, 2, pre);
+
+  VkImageResolve r = {};
+  r.srcSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
+  r.srcSubresource.layerCount = 1;
+  r.dstSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
+  r.dstSubresource.layerCount = 1;
+  r.extent = {width_, height_, 1};
+  command_buffer.CmdVkResolveImage(
+      color_image_, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL, resolve_image_,
+      VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL, 1, &r);
+
+  VkImageMemoryBarrier post = {};
+  post.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
+  post.srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT;
+  post.dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_READ_BIT;
+  post.oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL;
+  post.newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL;
+  post.srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  post.dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  post.image = resolve_image_;
+  post.subresourceRange = range;
+  command_buffer.CmdVkPipelineBarrier(VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT,
+                                      VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_FRAGMENT_SHADER_BIT, 0, 0,
+                                      nullptr, 0, nullptr, 1, &post);
+  return true;
+}
+
+bool BdNativeRenderer::StretchToPresent(DeferredCommandBuffer& command_buffer,
+                                        uint32_t src_width,
+                                        uint32_t src_height) {
+  if (present_image_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE || stretch_src_width_ == 0 ||
+      src_width == 0 || src_height == 0) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  VkImage src = (samples_ != VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT &&
+                 resolve_image_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE)
+                    ? resolve_image_
+                    : color_image_;
+  if (src == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  // The rendered image is SHADER_READ_ONLY (render pass finalLayout); present_image_
+  // is UNDEFINED (first) or SHADER_READ (prior frame). Transition src->TRANSFER_SRC,
+  // present->TRANSFER_DST, blit STRETCHED [0..src]->[0..present_width_], then both
+  // back to SHADER_READ for the gamma present pass.
+  VkImageSubresourceRange color_range = {};
+  color_range.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
+  color_range.levelCount = 1;
+  color_range.layerCount = 1;
+
+  VkImageMemoryBarrier pre[2] = {};
+  pre[0].sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
+  pre[0].srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_READ_BIT;
+  pre[0].dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_READ_BIT;
+  pre[0].oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL;
+  pre[0].newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL;
+  pre[0].srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  pre[0].dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  pre[0].image = src;
+  pre[0].subresourceRange = color_range;
+  pre[1] = pre[0];
+  pre[1].srcAccessMask = 0;
+  pre[1].dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT;
+  pre[1].oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
+  pre[1].newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL;
+  pre[1].image = present_image_;
+  command_buffer.CmdVkPipelineBarrier(
+      VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_FRAGMENT_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT,
+      VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT, 0, 0, nullptr, 0, nullptr, 2, pre);
+
+  VkImageBlit blit = {};
+  blit.srcSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
+  blit.srcSubresource.layerCount = 1;
+  blit.srcOffsets[0] = {0, 0, 0};
+  blit.srcOffsets[1] = {int32_t(src_width), int32_t(src_height), 1};
+  blit.dstSubresource.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
+  blit.dstSubresource.layerCount = 1;
+  blit.dstOffsets[0] = {0, 0, 0};
+  blit.dstOffsets[1] = {int32_t(present_width_), int32_t(height_), 1};
+  command_buffer.CmdVkBlitImage(
+      src, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL, present_image_,
+      VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL, 1, &blit, VK_FILTER_LINEAR);
+
+  VkImageMemoryBarrier post[2] = {};
+  post[0].sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_MEMORY_BARRIER;
+  post[0].srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_READ_BIT;
+  post[0].dstAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_SHADER_READ_BIT;
+  post[0].oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_SRC_OPTIMAL;
+  post[0].newLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL;
+  post[0].srcQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  post[0].dstQueueFamilyIndex = VK_QUEUE_FAMILY_IGNORED;
+  post[0].image = src;
+  post[0].subresourceRange = color_range;
+  post[1] = post[0];
+  post[1].srcAccessMask = VK_ACCESS_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT;
+  post[1].oldLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_TRANSFER_DST_OPTIMAL;
+  post[1].image = present_image_;
+  command_buffer.CmdVkPipelineBarrier(
+      VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_FRAGMENT_SHADER_BIT, 0,
+      0, nullptr, 0, nullptr, 2, post);
+  present_image_ready_ = true;
+  return true;
+}

   if (!initialized() || framebuffer_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
     return;
@@ -48,7 +220,7 @@ void BdNativeRenderer::RenderFrame(DeferredCommandBuffer& command_buffer) {

   VkClearValue clear_values[2] = {};
   clear_values[0].color = {{1.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f}};
-  clear_values[1].depthStencil = {0.0f, 0};

   VkRenderPassBeginInfo begin_info = {};

@@ -100,6 +272,261 @@ bool BdNativeRenderer::EnsureColorFormat(VkFormat color_format,

+// ---- Native-surface registry (real HLE / EDRAM-deletion substrate) ----------
+
+NativeSurface* BdNativeRenderer::FindSurface(uint32_t key) {
+  auto it = surfaces_.find(key);
+  return it == surfaces_.end() ? nullptr : &it->second;
+}
+
+VkImageView BdNativeRenderer::LookupSampledSurface(uint32_t guest_address) {
+  auto it = surfaces_.find(guest_address);
+  if (it == surfaces_.end()) {
+    return VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  // Only bind the native image once it holds valid rendered content (a completed
+  // render pass leaves it SHADER_READ_ONLY). Undefined => fall back to EDRAM.
+  if (it->second.color_layout != VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL) {
+    return VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  return it->second.color_view;
+}
+
+void BdNativeRenderer::BeginSurfaceFrame() {
+  for (auto& kv : surfaces_) {
+    kv.second.rendered_this_frame = false;
+  }
+}
+
+NativeSurface* BdNativeRenderer::AcquireSurface(uint32_t key, uint32_t width,
+                                                uint32_t height,
+                                                VkFormat color_format,
+                                                VkFormat depth_format,
+                                                VkSampleCountFlagBits samples) {
+  if (!key || !width || !height || color_format == VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED) {
+    return nullptr;
+  }
+  // MSAA aux surfaces need a resolve to be sampleable — not yet implemented.
+  if (samples != VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT) {
+    return nullptr;
+  }
+  auto it = surfaces_.find(key);
+  if (it != surfaces_.end()) {
+    NativeSurface& s = it->second;
+    // Reuse if the existing surface is ALREADY BIG ENOUGH + format-compatible.
+    // Requiring EXACT dims caused per-frame destroy/recreate churn (BD's scissor
+    // height oscillates 180<->184), which freed VkImages still in use by in-flight
+    // frames = use-after-free crash during scene transitions. A larger surface
+    // renders a smaller draw fine (viewport/scissor bound the area). Only GROW.
+    if (s.color_format == color_format && s.depth_format == depth_format &&
+        s.samples == samples && s.framebuffer != VK_NULL_HANDLE &&
+        s.width >= width && s.height >= height) {
+      return &s;
+    }
+    // Must grow/reformat: rebuild at the MAX of old+new dims so it stabilizes
+    // (never shrinks => no ping-pong recreation). Grows are one-time as dims
+    // settle, so the per-frame destroy/recreate churn (the crash trigger) stops.
+    width = s.width > width ? s.width : width;
+    height = s.height > height ? s.height : height;
+    DestroySurfaceResources(s);
+  }
+  NativeSurface& s = surfaces_[key];
+  s.key = key;
+  s.width = width;
+  s.height = height;
+  s.color_format = color_format;
+  s.depth_format =
+      depth_format == VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED ? VK_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT
+                                          : depth_format;
+  s.samples = samples;
+  s.color_layout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
+  s.rendered_this_frame = false;
+  if (!CreateSurfaceResources(s)) {
+    DestroySurfaceResources(s);
+    surfaces_.erase(key);
+    return nullptr;
+  }
+  static uint32_t s_logged = 0;
+  if (s_logged < 8) {
+    ++s_logged;
+    XELOGI(
+        "BdNativeRenderer: native surface key={:08X} {}x{} color={} depth={} "
+        "(live={})",
+        key, width, height, uint32_t(color_format), uint32_t(s.depth_format),
+        surfaces_.size());
+  }
+  return &s;
+}
+
+bool BdNativeRenderer::CreateSurfaceResources(NativeSurface& s) {
+  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* const vulkan_device =
+      command_processor_.GetVulkanDevice();
+  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = vulkan_device->functions();
+  const VkDevice device = vulkan_device->device();
+
+  // Color: SAMPLED (the field fetches it) + COLOR_ATTACHMENT (rendered into) +
+  // TRANSFER_SRC (debug/blit). Depth: attachment only.
+  VkImageCreateInfo ici = {};
+  ici.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_CREATE_INFO;
+  ici.imageType = VK_IMAGE_TYPE_2D;
+  ici.extent = {s.width, s.height, 1};
+  ici.mipLevels = 1;
+  ici.arrayLayers = 1;
+  ici.samples = s.samples;
+  ici.tiling = VK_IMAGE_TILING_OPTIMAL;
+  ici.sharingMode = VK_SHARING_MODE_EXCLUSIVE;
+  ici.initialLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
+
+  ici.format = s.color_format;
+  ici.usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_BIT |
+              VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_SRC_BIT;
+  if (!ui::vulkan::util::CreateDedicatedAllocationImage(
+          vulkan_device, ici, ui::vulkan::util::MemoryPurpose::kDeviceLocal,
+          s.color_image, s.color_memory)) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  ici.format = s.depth_format;
+  ici.usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_DEPTH_STENCIL_ATTACHMENT_BIT;
+  if (!ui::vulkan::util::CreateDedicatedAllocationImage(
+          vulkan_device, ici, ui::vulkan::util::MemoryPurpose::kDeviceLocal,
+          s.depth_image, s.depth_memory)) {
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  VkImageViewCreateInfo vci = {};
+  vci.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_IMAGE_VIEW_CREATE_INFO;
+  vci.viewType = VK_IMAGE_VIEW_TYPE_2D;
+  vci.subresourceRange.levelCount = 1;
+  vci.subresourceRange.layerCount = 1;
+  vci.image = s.color_image;
+  vci.format = s.color_format;
+  vci.subresourceRange.aspectMask = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT;
+  if (dfn.vkCreateImageView(device, &vci, nullptr, &s.color_view) !=
+      VK_SUCCESS) {
+    return false;
+  }
+  vci.image = s.depth_image;
+  vci.format = s.depth_format;
+  vci.subresourceRange.aspectMask =
+      VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_DEPTH_BIT | VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_STENCIL_BIT;
+  if (dfn.vkCreateImageView(device, &vci, nullptr, &s.depth_view) !=
+      VK_SUCCESS) {
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  // Two render passes (CLEAR first draw / LOAD accumulate) mirroring the primary
+  // field RT, but single-sample and with color finalLayout SHADER_READ so the
+  // surface is directly sampleable after rendering (that's the whole point —
+  // render-to-texture with no EDRAM resolve).
+  VkAttachmentDescription att[2] = {};
+  att[0].format = s.color_format;
+  att[0].samples = s.samples;
+  att[0].loadOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_LOAD_OP_CLEAR;
+  att[0].storeOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_STORE_OP_STORE;
+  att[0].stencilLoadOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_LOAD_OP_DONT_CARE;
+  att[0].stencilStoreOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_STORE_OP_DONT_CARE;
+  att[0].initialLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
+  att[0].finalLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL;
+  att[1].format = s.depth_format;
+  att[1].samples = s.samples;
+  att[1].loadOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_LOAD_OP_CLEAR;
+  att[1].storeOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_STORE_OP_STORE;
+  att[1].stencilLoadOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_LOAD_OP_DONT_CARE;
+  att[1].stencilStoreOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_STORE_OP_DONT_CARE;
+  att[1].initialLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
+  att[1].finalLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_DEPTH_STENCIL_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL;
+
+  VkAttachmentReference color_ref = {0,
+                                     VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL};
+  VkAttachmentReference depth_ref = {
+      1, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_DEPTH_STENCIL_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL};
+  VkSubpassDescription subpass = {};
+  subpass.pipelineBindPoint = VK_PIPELINE_BIND_POINT_GRAPHICS;
+  subpass.colorAttachmentCount = 1;
+  subpass.pColorAttachments = &color_ref;
+  subpass.pDepthStencilAttachment = &depth_ref;
+
+  VkRenderPassCreateInfo rpci = {};
+  rpci.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_RENDER_PASS_CREATE_INFO;
+  rpci.attachmentCount = 2;
+  rpci.pAttachments = att;
+  rpci.subpassCount = 1;
+  rpci.pSubpasses = &subpass;
+  if (dfn.vkCreateRenderPass(device, &rpci, nullptr, &s.render_pass_clear) !=
+      VK_SUCCESS) {
+    s.render_pass_clear = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+    return false;
+  }
+  att[0].loadOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_LOAD_OP_LOAD;
+  att[0].initialLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL;
+  att[1].loadOp = VK_ATTACHMENT_LOAD_OP_LOAD;
+  att[1].initialLayout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_DEPTH_STENCIL_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL;
+  if (dfn.vkCreateRenderPass(device, &rpci, nullptr, &s.render_pass_load) !=
+      VK_SUCCESS) {
+    s.render_pass_load = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+    return false;
+  }
+
+  VkImageView fb_attachments[2] = {s.color_view, s.depth_view};
+  VkFramebufferCreateInfo fbci = {};
+  fbci.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_FRAMEBUFFER_CREATE_INFO;
+  fbci.renderPass = s.render_pass_clear;
+  fbci.attachmentCount = 2;
+  fbci.pAttachments = fb_attachments;
+  fbci.width = s.width;
+  fbci.height = s.height;
+  fbci.layers = 1;
+  return dfn.vkCreateFramebuffer(device, &fbci, nullptr, &s.framebuffer) ==
+         VK_SUCCESS;
+}
+
+void BdNativeRenderer::DestroySurfaceResources(NativeSurface& s) {
+  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* const vulkan_device =
+      command_processor_.GetVulkanDevice();
+  if (!vulkan_device) {
+    return;
+  }
+  const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice::Functions& dfn = vulkan_device->functions();
+  const VkDevice device = vulkan_device->device();
+  if (s.framebuffer != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkDestroyFramebuffer(device, s.framebuffer, nullptr);
+    s.framebuffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.render_pass_load != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkDestroyRenderPass(device, s.render_pass_load, nullptr);
+    s.render_pass_load = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.render_pass_clear != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkDestroyRenderPass(device, s.render_pass_clear, nullptr);
+    s.render_pass_clear = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.color_view != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkDestroyImageView(device, s.color_view, nullptr);
+    s.color_view = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.color_image != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkDestroyImage(device, s.color_image, nullptr);
+    s.color_image = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.color_memory != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkFreeMemory(device, s.color_memory, nullptr);
+    s.color_memory = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.depth_view != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkDestroyImageView(device, s.depth_view, nullptr);
+    s.depth_view = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.depth_image != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkDestroyImage(device, s.depth_image, nullptr);
+    s.depth_image = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  if (s.depth_memory != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
+    dfn.vkFreeMemory(device, s.depth_memory, nullptr);
+    s.depth_memory = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
+  }
+  s.color_layout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
+}
+
 bool BdNativeRenderer::CreateImages() {
   const ui::vulkan::VulkanDevice* const vulkan_device =
       command_processor_.GetVulkanDevice();
@@ -188,6 +615,32 @@ bool BdNativeRenderer::CreateImages() {

     }

+
+  // Stretch present target (single-sample, full display width): the rendered field
+  // (left stretch_src_width_ px) is blitted STRETCHED into this on present so the
+  // narrower guest surface fills the display (BD upscales the field in its resolve).

+// THE real-HLE unit (EDRAM deletion): a single native render-to-texture surface
+// — its OWN persistent color(+depth) VkImage, keyed by the guest RESOLVE-
+// DESTINATION address (the stable D3D9 resource identity: the main-memory
+// texture the 360 surface resolves to and is LATER SAMPLED as). Because each
+// logical surface owns one image that is BOTH rendered into AND sampled, there
+// are NO EDRAM ownership transfers (the 35-pass / ~110ms wall). This replaces
+// xenia's EDRAM-base RenderTargetKey addressing with DXVK/Cemu-style
+// resource-identity addressing. Single-sample first (shadow/reflection RTs);
+// MSAA aux resolve is a follow-up.

# BD EDRAM-deletion Г+' 30fps: executable build plan (2026-07-08)

## The proven mechanism (why EDRAM deletion IS the 30fps path)
- BD's field is **GPU-fragment-bound**: 25.2fps, GPU 99% @ max clock 680MHz on the full BD GameProfile (VRS4 + fp10 + MSAA2 + clamp) + AOT. (profbound.ps1)
- The fragment cuts that reach 30fps **resize the render target** and **garble on the EDRAM tile model**:
  - MSAA 2Г+'1 (Г^'28%): 22.2fps + garble (profm1.ps1).
  - resolution downscale 85%: 26fps + heavy garble (profds.ps1).
  - Cause: BD's 2A--packed main-scene RT bases are re-derived wrong at reduced size in the EDRAM tile addressing (the "cap=1 footprint mismatch", bd-edram-atomic-rop-solve).
- Levers that DON'T resize the RT (VRS4, fp10) render clean but cap at 25fps.
- **Native resource-keyed RTs bypass EDRAM addressing Г+' the resized RTs render clean Г+' MSAA1/downscale unlock Г+' 30fps.** This is the confirmed reason to delete EDRAM.
- Dead ends (do not retry): naive early-Z `gpu_foliage_force_early_z` (22.7fps + white-band, profez.ps1); foliage decimation (user: never).

## What already works (PC-verified)
- Field renders **correct on desktop** with `gpu_bd_native_renderer` + `gpu_bd_native_aux_rt` + `gpu_bd_native_aux_fmt37=false` + `drop_resolves` + `drop_transfers` (desk_hle54.png, desk_mainscene.png). EDRAM resolves + color transfers deletable on PC, field correct.
- AOT object cache shipped default-on (EmulatorActivity.java onCreate ~line 207); warm launches skip the ~32% LLVM compile.
- `gpu_clamp_rt_image_height=768` (BD profile) clamps RT image to actual rows Г+' native-size RTs (tile-I/O minor).

## PRECISE PINNING (2026-07-08, desk_maxdel.png + desk_hle54.png)
- **Working HLE state = CORRECT at 46 passes on PC**: `gpu_bd_native_renderer` (fieldГ+'471 native, presented via present_output_view) + `gpu_bd_native_aux_rt aux_max_width=2048` (small RTs Г+' native surfaces keyed by resolve-dest) + drop_resolves + drop_transfers + drop_all_color_xfer. Field renders fully correct (desk_hle54.png). So: field + Г%2048px RTs are native, their EDRAM resolves + color transfers deleted.
- **CORRECTED (desk_covdiag.png, diagnostic build): the MAXIMAL EDRAM-op deletion renders CORRECT on PC.** Config = `gpu_native_render_targets` (independent RTs, no ownership transfers) + drop_resolves + drop_transfers + drop_all_color_xfer + aux (native surfaces) + `gpu_clamp_rt_image_height=768` + native renderer (fieldГ+'471). The earlier desk_maxdel.png "black" was a CAPTURE-TIMING transient, NOT a coverage gap Г?" the `gpu_bd_native_diag_coverage` diagnostic found ZERO >2048px RTs, and the identical config re-captured renders the full field correct. So there is NO wide-RT consume-side gap.
- **So the EXACT remaining EDRAM = the DEPTH transfers only** (drop_all_color_xfer keeps depth; depth is load-bearing, mixed-res 720Г+"400) + the structural RenderTargetKey EDRAM-base ENCODING (functional RTs are already native/independent). The EDRAM color ops + ownership model are DELETABLE on PC with the field correct Г?" verified.
- Гs Л,? Note a FLAKINESS: desk_maxdel black once, desk_covdiag correct Г?" a possible race in the native_render_targets + drop_all_color_xfer path worth confirming (capture 2-3A- on Thor).
- **DEPTH is the last load-bearing EDRAM op (desk_alldrop.png): dropping depth transfers too (`drop_all_xfer`) Г+' black even on the independent-RT config.** Consistent with every prior finding (depth is mixed-res 720Г+"400, carries load-bearing content between resolutions; drop Г+' 400-res depth empty Г+' geometry culled Г+' black). So the depth transfers CANNOT be dropped Г?" they need NATIVE mixed-res depth handling (render/convert depth on native RTs). That is the last EDRAM-op piece + the intricate part of the build. (Given the maxdel false-black, confirm desk_alldrop 2A- before treating as final, but it matches all prior depth results.)

## NATIVE-DEPTH BUILD SCOPE (the last EDRAM op, 2026-07-08)
The depth transfers go through xenia's `host_depth_store` COMPUTE shaders (`host_depth_store_{1x,2x,4x}msaa_cs`, vulkan_render_target_cache.cc ~276, HostDepthStoreConstants push-constants ~853). They convert BD's mixed-res depth (720Г+"400) via the EDRAM buffer Г?" GPU compute ops, not blits (which is why the depth CmdVkCopyImage path I added only handles same-layout). The native replacement: run the depth conversion between NATIVE depth RTs (or have the 400-res consumer sample the native 720 depth via the sampler at reduced res, eliminating the separate 400 buffer). This is the intricate SPIR-V/compute piece of the build. Files: the host_depth_store pipeline setup ~843-865, the transfer/copy block ~5912 (where I added is_depth CmdVkCopyImage + the drop filters).

## NET PC-FIRST STATE (2026-07-08): EDRAM COLOR ops are DELETABLE on PC with the field correct (resolves + color transfers + ownership all gone, desk_covdiag). The ONLY remaining EDRAM op is the DEPTH transfers (load-bearing, need native mixed-res depth). Plus the structural RenderTargetKey encoding. That is the precise, verified remaining scope.

## The build (in order)
1. **Main-scene native-RT coverage.** The aux path (`vulkan_command_processor.cc` ~4322) covers only resolve-dest surfaces (`aux_edge->dest_base`) and excludes Г%Э800px main-scene RTs (comment ~4345). The field renderer covers 471. Extend native coverage so the **main-scene color + depth** render into resource-keyed native VkImages (not EDRAM-tile-addressed), keyed by stable resource identity.
2. **Consume-side reader coverage.** The reason the main scene is excluded: its readers aren't redirected Г+' sampling the emptied EDRAM copy black-screens (A/B-proven on Thor; desktop already renders correct, so this gap is Turnip-specific). Extend `LookupSampledSurface`/Brick-B (rt_as_texture_views_pixel_, ~10903) AND the composite/present path (hybrid_postprocess composite passes ~2378/2499; `bd_present_native_total_` present path ~3394) to read the native surface for the main-scene RT.
3. **Fix the Turnip black-area gap** (present/composite on Turnip only Г?" desktop is clean).
4. **Then apply MSAA1 (or downscale) on the native RTs** and confirm clean render + 30fps on the Thor. This is the payoff and is Thor-only to validate (TBDR tile addressing is invisible on desktop immediate-mode).

## Validation protocol
- PC-first: each coverage step, verify field-correct via desk screenshot (run_cap.ps1, fmt37=false).
- Thor: single-run GPU-busy% + OSD fps (never cross-run Г?" scene-confounded). Cool <46A°C before each launch; force-stop >70A°C. Do NOT thrash (memory: never-thrash-thor).
- Success = main scene native + MSAA1 clean + fps Г%Э30 single-run.

- `src/xenia/gpu/vulkan/vulkan_command_processor.cc`: aux gate ~4322-4388; Brick-B ~10903; present ~3394; composite ~2378-2612.

- Profile: `GameProfiles.java` "4D5307DF".
- Memory: [[bd-field-cpu-bound-not-gpu]] (has the full corrected synthesis + all dead-end tests).

    35: // THE real-HLE unit (EDRAM deletion): a single native render-to-texture surface
    36: // ƒ?" its OWN persistent color(+depth) VkImage, keyed by the guest RESOLVE-
    37: // DESTINATION address (the stable D3D9 resource identity: the main-memory
    38: // texture the 360 surface resolves to and is LATER SAMPLED as). Because each
    39: // logical surface owns one image that is BOTH rendered into AND sampled, there
    40: // are NO EDRAM ownership transfers (the 35-pass / ~110ms wall). This replaces
    41: // xenia's EDRAM-base RenderTargetKey addressing with DXVK/Cemu-style
    42: // resource-identity addressing. Single-sample first (shadow/reflection RTs);
    43: // MSAA aux resolve is a follow-up.
    44: struct NativeSurface {
    45:   uint32_t key = 0;  // resolve-dest guest address = resource identity
    46:   uint32_t width = 0;
    47:   uint32_t height = 0;
    48:   VkFormat color_format = VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED;
    49:   VkFormat depth_format = VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED;
    50:   VkSampleCountFlagBits samples = VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT;
    51:   VkImage color_image = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    52:   VkDeviceMemory color_memory = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    53:   VkImageView color_view = VK_NULL_HANDLE;  // SAMPLED + COLOR_ATTACHMENT
    54:   VkImage depth_image = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    55:   VkDeviceMemory depth_memory = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    56:   VkImageView depth_view = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    57:   VkRenderPass render_pass_clear = VK_NULL_HANDLE;  // first draw into this surface
    58:   VkRenderPass render_pass_load = VK_NULL_HANDLE;   // accumulate re-begins
    59:   VkFramebuffer framebuffer = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
    60:   // Tracks the color image's current layout so binds/attaches barrier correctly.
    61:   VkImageLayout color_layout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
    62:   bool rendered_this_frame = false;  // false => next attach uses the CLEAR pass
    63: };
    64: 
    65: // Brick 1: owns a persistent native full-surface color+depth RT + one render
    66: // pass. Bricks 2-4 add native pipelines (VkBuffer vertex-input, Xenos->SPIR-V,
    67: // hardware ROP blend), the captured-draw submit loop, depth-prepass + early-Z,
    68: // and composite folding. Kept deliberately separate from RenderTargetCache so it
    69: // does NOT go through the EDRAM tile/transfer/resolve machinery.
    70: class BdNativeRenderer {
    71:  public:
    72:   explicit BdNativeRenderer(VulkanCommandProcessor& command_processor)
    73:       : command_processor_(command_processor) {}
    74:   ~BdNativeRenderer() { Shutdown(); }
    75: 
    76:   // Allocate the persistent color+depth image + render pass + framebuffer, sized
    77:   // to BD's full field surface (default 720x1280; the real dims come from the
    78:   // captured surface state). Returns false on failure (caller falls back to LLE).
    79:   // stretch_src_width: when >0, the field renders into the left stretch_src_width
    80:   // px and is blitted STRETCHED to the full width on present (fill the screen);
    81:   // 0 = present the rendered region as-is.
    82:   bool Initialize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height, uint32_t stretch_src_width = 0);
    83:   void Shutdown();
    84: 
    85:   // Recreate the color image + view + render pass + framebuffer for `format` if
    86:   // it differs from the current one - so xenia's field pipelines (built for the
    87:   // field's color format) are render-pass-COMPATIBLE with the native pass (else
    88:   // "device lost"). Call BEFORE redirecting a field draw (pass not yet begun, so
    89:   // the images aren't in use). Returns false on failure.
    90:   bool EnsureColorFormat(VkFormat color_format, VkFormat depth_format,
    91:                          VkSampleCountFlagBits samples);
    92: 
    93:   // Brick 2a: begin the ONE held-open native render pass on `command_buffer`
    94:   // (clears color+depth), then EndRenderPass. Bricks 2b-3 record the captured
    95:   // 0x82489F40 draws (native pipelines + vertex-input) BETWEEN begin and end.
    96:   // Leaves color_image_ in SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL (finalLayout) for present.
    97:   // Uses xenia's DeferredCommandBuffer (recorded on the CP thread, replayed on
    98:   // the worker) - NOT a raw VkCommandBuffer - so it slots into the frame flow.
    99:   void RenderFrame(DeferredCommandBuffer& command_buffer);
   100: 
   101:   bool initialized() const { return render_pass_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE; }
   102:   VkImage color_image() const { return color_image_; }
   103:   // Sampled color view to present. When the field is MSAA (samples_ > 1) the
   104:   // render pass resolves color into a SINGLE-SAMPLE resolve image - present THAT
   105:   // (an MSAA image sampled directly bands). Single-sample: present color directly.
   106:   VkImageView color_view() const {
   107:     return (samples_ != VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT && resolve_view_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE)
   108:                ? resolve_view_
   109:                : color_view_;
   110:   }
   111:   // The view to PRESENT: the stretched present image when stretch is active + ready,
   112:   // else the rendered image directly (color_view()).
   113:   VkImageView present_output_view() const {
   114:     if (stretch_src_width_ > 0 && present_view_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE &&
   115:         present_image_ready_) {
   116:       return present_view_;
   117:     }
   118:     return color_view();
   119:   }
   120:   // The single-sample image holding the rendered field (resolve when MSAA, else
   121:   // color) - the SOURCE for the stretch blit.
   122:   VkImage rendered_image() const {
   123:     return (samples_ != VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT && resolve_image_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE)
   124:                ? resolve_image_
   125:                : color_image_;
   126:   }
   127:   // BD field-fill STRETCH: the bin-once renders the whole field into the left
   128:   // src_width px of the native RT (the field's guest surface is narrower than the
   129:   // 1280 display - BD upscales it in the resolve). Blit that region to the full
   130:   // present_image_ width so the field fills the screen, then present present_image_.
   131:   // No-op (returns false) if the present image isn't allocated (stretch disabled).
   132:   bool StretchToPresent(DeferredCommandBuffer& command_buffer, uint32_t src_width,
   133:                         uint32_t src_height);
   134:   // ADRENO: the render pass has no in-pass resolve (for pipeline compat), so resolve
   135:   // the MSAA color -> single-sample resolve_image_ SEPARATELY before present. No-op
   136:   // on desktop (in-pass resolve) or single-sample. Call before present/StretchToPresent.
   137:   bool ResolveMsaa(DeferredCommandBuffer& command_buffer);
   138:   bool has_present_image() const { return present_image_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE; }

   282: VkImageView BdNativeRenderer::LookupSampledSurface(uint32_t guest_address) {
   283:   auto it = surfaces_.find(guest_address);

   295: void BdNativeRenderer::BeginSurfaceFrame() {
   296:   for (auto& kv : surfaces_) {

   301: NativeSurface* BdNativeRenderer::AcquireSurface(uint32_t key, uint32_t width,
   302:                                                 uint32_t height,
   303:                                                 VkFormat color_format,
   304:                                                 VkFormat depth_format,
   305:                                                 VkSampleCountFlagBits samples) {
   306:   if (!key || !width || !height || color_format == VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED) {
   307:     return nullptr;
   308:   }
   309:   // MSAA aux surfaces need a resolve to be sampleable ƒ?" not yet implemented.
   310:   if (samples != VK_SAMPLE_COUNT_1_BIT) {
   311:     return nullptr;
   312:   }
   313:   auto it = surfaces_.find(key);
   314:   if (it != surfaces_.end()) {
   315:     NativeSurface& s = it->second;
   316:     // Reuse if the existing surface is ALREADY BIG ENOUGH + format-compatible.
   317:     // Requiring EXACT dims caused per-frame destroy/recreate churn (BD's scissor
   318:     // height oscillates 180<->184), which freed VkImages still in use by in-flight
   319:     // frames = use-after-free crash during scene transitions. A larger surface
   320:     // renders a smaller draw fine (viewport/scissor bound the area). Only GROW.
   321:     if (s.color_format == color_format && s.depth_format == depth_format &&
   322:         s.samples == samples && s.framebuffer != VK_NULL_HANDLE &&
   323:         s.width >= width && s.height >= height) {
   324:       return &s;
   325:     }
   326:     // Must grow/reformat: rebuild at the MAX of old+new dims so it stabilizes
   327:     // (never shrinks => no ping-pong recreation). Grows are one-time as dims
   328:     // settle, so the per-frame destroy/recreate churn (the crash trigger) stops.
   329:     width = s.width > width ? s.width : width;
   330:     height = s.height > height ? s.height : height;
   331:     DestroySurfaceResources(s);
   332:   }
   333:   NativeSurface& s = surfaces_[key];
   334:   s.key = key;
   335:   s.width = width;
   336:   s.height = height;
   337:   s.color_format = color_format;
   338:   s.depth_format =
   339:       depth_format == VK_FORMAT_UNDEFINED ? VK_FORMAT_D24_UNORM_S8_UINT
   340:                                           : depth_format;
   341:   s.samples = samples;
   342:   s.color_layout = VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_UNDEFINED;
   343:   s.rendered_this_frame = false;
   344:   if (!CreateSurfaceResources(s)) {
   345:     DestroySurfaceResources(s);
   346:     surfaces_.erase(key);
   347:     return nullptr;
   348:   }
   349:   static uint32_t s_logged = 0;
   350:   if (s_logged < 8) {
   351:     ++s_logged;
   352:     XELOGI(
   353:         "BdNativeRenderer: native surface key={:08X} {}x{} color={} depth={} "
   354:         "(live={})",
   355:         key, width, height, uint32_t(color_format), uint32_t(s.depth_format),
   356:         surfaces_.size());
   357:   }
   358:   return &s;
   359: }

   438: 
   439:   VkAttachmentReference color_ref = {0,
   440:                                      VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_COLOR_ATTACHMENT_OPTIMAL};
   441:   VkAttachmentReference depth_ref = {

 11397:   // Reuse texture and sampler bindings if not changed since the last draw
 11398:   // (vulkan_cache_texture_descriptors). Allocating a transient descriptor set
 11399:   // and calling vkUpdateDescriptorSets for the textures/samplers on every draw
 11400:   // is the dominant CPU cost on the Adreno command thread; most consecutive
 11401:   // draws bind the exact same image views and samplers, so a fresh write is
 11402:   // wasted work. Build a signature of the would-be contents (image views and
 11403:   // samplers, plus counts which encode the descriptor set layout) and only mark
 11404:   // the descriptor set out of date - forcing the existing rewrite path below -
 11405:   // when the signature actually changes. The signature stores exact handles
 11406:   // (not a hash), so it is precise. When caching is disabled, fall back to the
 11407:   // original unconditional rewrite.
 11408:   if (native_render_path_active_) {
 11409:     // BRICK 1: texture/sampler slots were pushed as push constants above and the
 11410:     // global bindless set is bound once - nothing to sign, allocate, or write
 11411:     // here, and the set-2/set-3 value bits stay up-to-date (do NOT fall into the
 11412:     // else that clears them).
 11413:   } else if (cvars::vulkan_cache_texture_descriptors) {
 11414:     auto build_texture_signature =
 11415:         [this](bool is_vertex, uint32_t texture_count, uint32_t sampler_count,
 11416:                const std::vector<VulkanShader::TextureBinding>* textures,
 11417:                const std::vector<
 11418:                    std::pair<VulkanTextureCache::SamplerParameters, VkSampler>>&
 11419:                    samplers,
 11420:                std::vector<uint64_t>& signature_out) {
 11421:           signature_out.clear();
 11422:           signature_out.push_back((uint64_t(texture_count) << 32) |
 11423:                                   uint64_t(sampler_count));
 11424:           if (texture_count && textures) {
 11425:             for (const VulkanShader::TextureBinding& texture_binding :
 11426:                  *textures) {

 11428:               // match the descriptor write loop below exactly, or the descriptor
 11429:               // cache keeps a stale view. Vertex textures are never overridden.
 11430:               VkImageView view =
 11431:                   is_vertex
 11432:                       ? VK_NULL_HANDLE
 11433:                       : rt_as_texture_views_pixel_[texture_binding
 11434:                                                        .fetch_constant];
 11435:               if (view == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
 11436:                 view = (!is_vertex && feedback_merge_active_)
 11437:                            ? feedback_merge_producer_view_
 11438:                            : texture_cache_->GetActiveBindingOrNullImageView(
 11439:                                  texture_binding.fetch_constant,
 11440:                                  texture_binding.dimension,
 11441:                                  bool(texture_binding.is_signed));
 11442:               }
 11443:               signature_out.push_back(reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(view));
 11444:             }
 11445:           }
 11446:           if (sampler_count) {
 11447:             for (const std::pair<VulkanTextureCache::SamplerParameters,
 11448:                                  VkSampler>& sampler_pair : samplers) {
 11449:               signature_out.push_back(
 11450:                   reinterpret_cast<uint64_t>(sampler_pair.second));
 11451:             }
 11452:           }
 11453:         };
 11454:     // Vertex/pixel textures/samplers. Build the signature into a REUSED scratch
 11455:     // buffer (build_texture_signature clears it but keeps its capacity) instead
 11456:     // of a fresh per-draw heap vector, and swap it into the stored signature on
 11457:     // a mismatch so the old buffer's capacity is recycled. Device-profiled
 11458:     // 2026-06-24: the per-draw std::vector alloc/realloc/free here (push_back ->
 11459:     // __push_back_slow_path -> __split_buffer) was a hot cost on the GPU
 11460:     // command-processor draw path. Behaviorally identical (the stored signatures
 11461:     // are the same); only the per-draw allocation is removed.

  5025: void VulkanCommandProcessor::FinalizeBdNativeColorMirrorAfterPass() {
  5026:   if (!bd_color_mirror_active_) {
  5027:     return;
  5028:   }
  5029:   // Clear the arm FIRST: MirrorBdNativeColorProducer -> SubmitBarriers ->
  5030:   // EndRenderPass re-enters this function; the false arm makes it a no-op (no
  5031:   // recursion, exactly one mirror per producer pass).
  5032:   bd_color_mirror_active_ = false;
  5033:   const VulkanRenderTargetCache::Framebuffer* fb = bd_color_mirror_fb_;
  5034:   bd_color_mirror_fb_ = nullptr;
  5035:   if (!fb) {
  5036:     return;
  5037:   }
  5038:   // The producer pass is already ended in the recorded stream. The mirror's
  5039:   // barriers + vkCmdCopyImage must not be seen as inside a pass, so temporarily
  5040:   // clear the tracker (the internal SubmitBarriers would otherwise emit a
  5041:   // spurious CmdVkEndRenderPass), then restore it for the caller.
  5042:   VkRenderPass saved_render_pass = current_render_pass_;
  5043:   current_render_pass_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  5044:   render_target_cache_->MirrorBdNativeColorProducer(fb);
  5045:   current_render_pass_ = saved_render_pass;
  5046:   // LEVEL 5: record this finalized native producer as the latest generation, so
  5047:   // the next fullscreen resolve can publish it as the alias for whichever
  5048:   // frontbuffer base it writes. Mirror left the native image SHADER_READ-ready.
  5049:   if (cvars::gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle >= 5 &&
  5050:       fb->bd_native_color_view != VK_NULL_HANDLE &&
  5051:       fb->bd_native_color_lle_rt) {
  5052:     uint32_t src_rt_key = fb->bd_native_color_lle_rt->key().key;
  5053:     BdL5Alias entry;
  5054:     entry.view = fb->bd_native_color_view;
  5055:     entry.fb = fb;
  5056:     entry.generation = ++bd_l5_generation_counter_;
  5057:     entry.frame_epoch = bd_l5_frame_epoch_;
  5058:     entry.width = fb->host_extent.width;
  5059:     entry.height = fb->host_extent.height;
  5060:     entry.src_rt_key = src_rt_key;
  5061:     bd_l5_producer_by_srckey_[src_rt_key] = entry;
  5062:   }
  5063: }
  5064: 
  5065: void VulkanCommandProcessor::EndRenderPass() {
  5066:   assert_true(submission_open_);
  5067:   if (current_render_pass_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
  5068:     return;
  5069:   }
  5070:   // gpu_vulkan_retro_depth_none: hindsight depth-none patch for the ending pass.
  5071:   RetroPatchDepthNoneAtPassEnd();
  5072:   // Lever 2 (vulkan_merge_draws): the pending draw-concatenation run's draws
  5073:   // belong to THIS render pass - realize them before it ends. This is the master
  5074:   // pass-end flush (covers SubmitBarriers force/barrier breaks and the IssueCopy/
  5075:   // IssueSwap/EndSubmission pass teardowns). No-op when no run is pending, and
  5076:   // never called between mergeable draws (the pass stays open), so coalescing is
  8516:         }
  8517:         if (stript_mergeable) {
  8518:           merge_stript_run_active_ = true;
  8519:           merge_stript_run_len_ = 1;
  8520:           merge_stript_run_pipeline_ = current_guest_graphics_pipeline_;
  8521:           merge_stript_run_layout_ = current_guest_graphics_pipeline_layout_;
  8522:           merge_stript_run_vgt_offset_ = stript_vgt_offset;
  8523:         } else {
  8524:           merge_stript_run_active_ = false;
  8525:           merge_stript_run_len_ = 0;
  8526:         }
  8527:       }
  8528:     }
  8529:   }
  8530:   trace_last_draw_sequence_ = ++trace_draw_sequence_;
  8531:   trace_last_draw_vs_hash_ = vertex_shader_hash;
  8532:   trace_last_draw_ps_hash_ = pixel_shader_hash;
  8533: 
  8534:   // Invalidate textures in memexported memory and watch for changes.
  8535:   for (const draw_util::MemExportRange& memexport_range : memexport_ranges_) {
  8536:     shared_memory_->RangeWrittenByGpu(memexport_range.base_address_dwords << 2,
  8537:                                       memexport_range.size_bytes);
  8538:   }
  8539: 
  8540:   return true;
  8541: }
  8542: 
  8543: bool VulkanCommandProcessor::BdNativeSurfaceServes(uint32_t dest_base) {
  8544:   return cvars::gpu_bd_native_aux_rt && bd_native_renderer_ &&
  8545:          bd_native_renderer_->LookupSampledSurface(dest_base) != VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  8546: }
  8547: 
  8548: void VulkanCommandProcessor::BdNoteColorConsumer(uint32_t dest_base,
  8549:                                                  uint32_t consumer) {
  8550:   if (cvars::gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle <= 0 || !dest_base) {
  8551:     return;
  8552:   }
  8553:   bd_color_consumer_bits_[dest_base] |= consumer;
  8554: }
  8555: 
  8556: void VulkanCommandProcessor::BdL5PublishAlias(uint32_t dest_base,
  8557:                                               uint32_t src_rt_key, uint32_t width,
  8558:                                               uint32_t height) {
  8559:   if (cvars::gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle < 5 || !dest_base || !src_rt_key) {
  8560:     return;
  8561:   }
  8562:   // Match the producer whose SOURCE RT key equals this resolve's source (the
  8563:   // native image that actually holds this dest's content). Only if finalized
  8564:   // THIS epoch (content live).
  8565:   auto it = bd_l5_producer_by_srckey_.find(src_rt_key);
  8566:   if (it == bd_l5_producer_by_srckey_.end() ||
  8567:       it->second.view == VK_NULL_HANDLE ||
  8568:       it->second.frame_epoch != bd_l5_frame_epoch_) {
  8569:     return;
  8570:   }
  8571:   BdL5Alias alias = it->second;
  8572:   alias.width = width;
  8573:   alias.height = height;
  8574:   bd_l5_alias_by_dest_[dest_base] = alias;
  8575:   if (xe::Clock::QueryGuestUptimeMillis() > 135000) {
  8576:     static std::atomic<uint32_t> s_pub{0};
  8577:     if (s_pub.fetch_add(1) < 40) {
  8578:       XELOGI("L5 ALIAS dest={:08X} src={:08X} gen={} epoch={} {}x{}", dest_base,
  8579:              alias.src_rt_key, alias.generation, alias.frame_epoch, width,
  8580:              height);
  8581:     }
  8582:   }
  8583: }
  8584: 
  8585: VkImageView VulkanCommandProcessor::BdL5LookupAlias(uint32_t guest_base) {
  8586:   if (cvars::gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle < 5 || !guest_base) {
  8587:     return VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  8588:   }
  8589:   auto it = bd_l5_alias_by_dest_.find(guest_base);
  8590:   if (it == bd_l5_alias_by_dest_.end() ||
  8591:       it->second.view == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
  8592:     return VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  8593:   }
  8594:   // Serve only aliases published in the CURRENT epoch (the double-buffered
  8595:   // frontbuffer: last frame's other-base entry is stale).
  8596:   if (it->second.frame_epoch != bd_l5_frame_epoch_) {
  8597:     return VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  8598:   }
  8599:   return it->second.view;
  8600: }
  8601: 
  8602: bool VulkanCommandProcessor::BdL5DropSafe(uint32_t dest_base) {
  8603:   if (cvars::gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle < 6 || !dest_base) {
  8604:     return false;
  8605:   }
  8606:   // A live native alias must cover this dest (present/sampler reads native).
  8607:   if (BdL5LookupAlias(dest_base) == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
  8608:     return false;
  8609:   }
  8610:   // Every consumer of this dest LAST frame must have been native (present /
  8611:   // pixel-texture / composite), with NO NonNative reader - else dropping the
  8612:   // EDRAM transfer starves a consumer that still reads the LLE surface.
  8613:   auto it = bd_color_consumer_bits_prev_.find(dest_base);
  8614:   if (it == bd_color_consumer_bits_prev_.end()) {
  8615:     return false;
  8616:   }
  8617:   uint32_t bits = it->second;
  8618:   if (bits & kBdConsumerNonNative) {
  8619:     return false;
  8620:   }
  8621:   return (bits & (kBdConsumerPresent | kBdConsumerPixelTexture |
  8622:                   kBdConsumerComposite)) != 0;
  8623: }

  4571:         current_framebuffer_->color_view != framebuffer->color_view;

  4574:         "extent_match={} distinct={} texcount={}",
  4575:         current_framebuffer_->host_extent.width,
  4576:         current_framebuffer_->host_extent.height,
  4577:         reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(current_framebuffer_->color_view),
  4578:         framebuffer->host_extent.width, framebuffer->host_extent.height,
  4579:         reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(framebuffer->color_view), feedback_extent_match,
  4580:         feedback_distinct_views, feedback_merge_texcount_);
  4581:     // In-place feedback (producer == consumer RT) uses the 1-attachment self-
  4582:     // dependency variant; distinct producer/consumer RTs use the 2-attachment
  4583:     // variant. BD's composites are all in-place.

  4600:       FlushPendingMergeRun();

  4614:       feedback_producer_begin_pos_ = SIZE_MAX;
  4615:       ++rt_feedback_merges_;
  4616:       return;
  4617:     }
  4618:   }
  4619:   // Instrumentation: attribute per-draw render-pass breaks. A break here is
  4620:   // caused either by SubmitBarriers ending the pass for a pending barrier, or by
  4621:   // the render pass / framebuffer changing (RT reconfiguration).
  4622:   bool was_in_render_pass = current_render_pass_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  4623:   SubmitBarriers(false);
  4624:   if (was_in_render_pass && current_render_pass_ == VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
  4625:     // SubmitBarriers ended the pass to emit a pending barrier.
  4626:     ++rt_pass_break_barrier_;
  4627:   }
  4628:   if (current_render_pass_ == render_pass &&
  4629:       current_framebuffer_ == framebuffer) {
  4630:     return;
  4631:   }
  4632:   if (current_render_pass_ != VK_NULL_HANDLE) {
  4633:     // Ending because the render pass / framebuffer changed (RT reconfig).
  4634:     // Lever 2: this direct CmdVkEndRenderPass bypasses EndRenderPass(), so flush
  4635:     // the pending concatenation run (it belongs to the old pass) before ending it.
  4636:     FlushPendingMergeRun();
  4637:     ++rt_pass_break_rt_change_;
  4638:     // gpu_vulkan_retro_depth_none: this direct end bypasses EndRenderPass() -
  4639:     // apply the hindsight depth-none patch for the ending pass here too.
  4640:     RetroPatchDepthNoneAtPassEnd();

  4642:     // End-of-pass timestamp AFTER EndRenderPass so it captures the TBDR tile
  4643:     // store/flush (the deferred binning+fragment work), not just draw recording.
  4644:     RecordPassTimestamp(false);
  4645:     // gpu_trace_resolve_timing: identify the small-draw oversized-RT pass that
  4646:     // just ended (current_pass_kind_ / current_framebuffer_ still the old pass).
  4647:     MaybeLogSmallGuestPass();
  4648:     // LEVEL 4: this direct end bypasses EndRenderPass() - mirror the just-ended
  4649:     // producer's native color -> LLE here too (before the next pass, which may
  4650:     // resolve/transfer it). Finalize saves/nulls/restores current_render_pass_
  4651:     // internally, so the still-old tracker value is safe.
  4652:     FinalizeBdNativeColorMirrorAfterPass();
  4653:   }
  4654:   current_render_pass_ = render_pass;
  4655:   current_framebuffer_ = framebuffer;
  4656:   // LEVEL 4 color-only native HLE (gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle >= 4): if
  4657:   // this guest pass's framebuffer carries a private native color producer, SEED
  4658:   // it (LLE color -> native) now - BEFORE BeginRenderPass is recorded, while no
  4659:   // pass is open - substitute the color attachment to the native image, and arm
  4660:   // the pass-end mirror. Correctness-neutral round-trip (seeds in, mirrors out;
  4661:   // drops nothing). kGuest only (the field producer); composites/transfers keep
  4662:   // the plain LLE framebuffer.
  4663:   bd_color_mirror_active_ = false;
  4664:   bd_color_mirror_fb_ = nullptr;
  4665:   // kGuest catches the pure-LLE field producer (471); kGuestComposite catches the
  4666:   // final 1280-wide COMPOSITE producer in the gpu_bd_native_renderer config (where
  4667:   // the field goes native + bypasses this hook). The RTC's width>=1024 gate on

  4669:   // (bloom pyramid + shadow maps are excluded), so bd_native_color_framebuffer!=NULL
  4670:   // means this IS that surface.
  4671:   // NARROW-THE-SUBSTITUTION (>=5): only substitute the LEARNED frontbuffer
  4672:   // producer (the RT key that resolves to a presented base). Substituting every
  4673:   // wide composite blacks the accumulated HDR->LDR chain (RenderDoc-proven).
  4674:   // At ==4 (round-trip validation) keep the broad behavior (mirror keeps LLE
  4675:   // correct). Until a producer is learned, substitute nothing at >=5 (LLE present).
  4676:   // The narrowing existed to tame the native_renderer config's own framing bug.
  4677:   // On PURE-LLE (native_renderer off) the field renders correct, so substitute
  4678:   // BROADLY (field 471 + composite both get native producers + published aliases)
  4679:   // to enable upstream coverage (redirect the composite's field sampler -> drop
  4680:   // the field->composite transfer). Narrow ONLY when native_renderer is on.
  4681:   bool l5_allowed = true;
  4682:   if (cvars::gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle >= 5 &&
  4683:       cvars::gpu_bd_native_renderer) {
  4684:     uint32_t l5_key = (framebuffer && framebuffer->bd_native_color_lle_rt)
  4685:                           ? framebuffer->bd_native_color_lle_rt->key().key
  4686:                           : 0u;
  4687:     l5_allowed = l5_key && bd_l5_allowed_producer_keys_.count(l5_key) != 0;
  4688:   }
  4689:   if (cvars::gpu_bd_native_color_lifetime_hle >= 4 &&
  4690:       (pass_kind == GpuPassKind::kGuest ||
  4691:        pass_kind == GpuPassKind::kGuestComposite) &&
  4692:       framebuffer &&
  4693:       framebuffer->bd_native_color_framebuffer != VK_NULL_HANDLE && l5_allowed) {
  4694:     // No pass is open in the recorded stream here (the old pass, if any, was
  4695:     // ended above; the new one is not begun yet). current_render_pass_ was just
  4696:     // re-pointed to the NEW pass for tracking, but the seed's SubmitBarriers
  4697:     // would misread that as an open pass and emit a spurious CmdVkEndRenderPass -
  4698:     // so null the tracker across the seed, then restore it.
  4699:     current_render_pass_ = VK_NULL_HANDLE;
  4700:     if (render_target_cache_->SeedBdNativeColorProducer(framebuffer)) {
  4701:       bd_color_mirror_active_ = true;
  4702:       bd_color_mirror_fb_ = framebuffer;
  4703:     }
  4704:     current_render_pass_ = render_pass;
  4705:   }
  4706:   // Safe DONT_CARE: if the draw opening this pass provably overwrites the
  4707:   // whole render area for some attachments, begin with a load-DONT_CARE
  4708:   // variant (compatible - load/store ops don't affect render pass
  4709:   // compatibility, so pipelines and the framebuffer remain valid, and
  4710:   // current_render_pass_ keeps tracking the original for the resume compare).
  4711:   VkRenderPass begin_render_pass = render_pass;
  4712:   // gpu_vulkan_skip_unused_depth_store: when the draw opening this GUEST pass
  4713:   // provably never touches depth/stencil, begin the depth attachment with
  4714:   // loadOp=DONT_CARE + storeOp=NONE (skips its oversized tile load+store while
  4715:   // preserving the depth EDRAM memory). Guarded to guest passes so transfer /
  4716:   // resolve passes - which DO write their attachment - stay normal. The draw path
  4717:   // breaks this pass before any depth-using draw, so the attachment is never
  4718:   // accessed (STORE_OP_NONE validity) and its memory is reloaded by that next
  4719:   // normal pass. Same-variant compatibility as the dc_safe variant above.
  4720:   current_pass_depth_store_none_ = false;
  4721:   if (cvars::gpu_vulkan_skip_unused_depth_store && depth_store_none_pending_ &&
  4722:       pass_kind == GpuPassKind::kGuest) {
  4723:     VkRenderPass depth_none_variant =
  4724:         render_target_cache_->GetDepthStoreNoneVariantForLastUpdate(render_pass);
  4725:     if (depth_none_variant != render_pass) {
  4726:       begin_render_pass = depth_none_variant;
  4727:       current_pass_depth_store_none_ = true;
  4728:       ++draw_outcomes_depth_none_passes_;
  4729:     }
  4730:   }
  4731:   if (begin_render_pass == render_pass && dc_safe_pending_state_mask_) {
  4732:     const VkExtent2D& dc_safe_extent = framebuffer->host_extent;
  4733:     if (dc_safe_pending_rect_[0] <= 0 && dc_safe_pending_rect_[1] <= 0 &&
  4734:         dc_safe_pending_rect_[2] >= int32_t(dc_safe_extent.width) &&
  4735:         dc_safe_pending_rect_[3] >= int32_t(dc_safe_extent.height)) {
  4736:       VkRenderPass dc_safe_variant =
  4737:           render_target_cache_->GetLoadDontCareVariantForLastUpdate(
  4738:               render_pass, dc_safe_pending_state_mask_);
  4739:       if (dc_safe_variant != render_pass) {
  4740:         begin_render_pass = dc_safe_variant;
  4741:         ++draw_outcomes_dc_safe_passes_;
  4742:         draw_outcomes_dc_safe_atts_ += xe::bit_count(
  4743:             dc_safe_pending_state_mask_ &
  4744:             render_target_cache_->last_update_render_pass_key()
  4745:                 .depth_and_color_used);
  4746:       }
  4747:     }
  4748:   }
  4749:   VkRenderPassBeginInfo render_pass_begin_info;
  4750:   render_pass_begin_info.sType = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_RENDER_PASS_BEGIN_INFO;
  4751:   render_pass_begin_info.pNext = nullptr;
  4752:   render_pass_begin_info.renderPass = begin_render_pass;
  4753:   // LEVEL 4: render into the native color image (LLE depth kept) when armed.
  4754:   render_pass_begin_info.framebuffer =
  4755:       bd_color_mirror_active_ ? framebuffer->bd_native_color_framebuffer
  4756:                               : framebuffer->framebuffer;
  4757:   render_pass_begin_info.renderArea.offset.x = 0;
  4758:   render_pass_begin_info.renderArea.offset.y = 0;
  4759:   // TODO(Triang3l): Actual dirty width / height in the deferred command
  4760:   // buffer.
  4761:   render_pass_begin_info.renderArea.extent = framebuffer->host_extent;
  4762:   // BD-30 lever: the EDRAM RT cache allocates host RTs at the EDRAM-tile-rounded
  4763:   // height (1280x720 guest -> 1280x2048 host, some up to x8192), and renderArea =
  4764:   // full host_extent makes the Adreno TBDR load/store/bin the oversized off-screen
  4765:   // rows every pass = the dominant GPU emulation overhead (per-pass timestamps:
  4766:   // a 1-draw pass over a 720x1824 RT cost 51ms). Clamp renderArea to the guest
  4767:   // scissor's max extent so only the active region is tiled. Gated for safe A/B;
  4768:   // validate render correctness (a too-small renderArea would clip later draws).
  4769:   if (cvars::gpu_clamp_renderarea_to_scissor) {
  4770:     draw_util::Scissor scissor;
  4771:     draw_util::GetScissor(*register_file_, scissor);
  4772:     uint32_t max_x = scissor.offset[0] + scissor.extent[0];
  4773:     uint32_t max_y = scissor.offset[1] + scissor.extent[1];
  4774:     if (max_x > 0 && max_x < render_pass_begin_info.renderArea.extent.width) {
  4775:       render_pass_begin_info.renderArea.extent.width = max_x;
  4776:     }
